<h1>Why Choose 1st Source?</h1>
<p>At 1st Source, our mission is to help our clients achieve security, build wealth and realize their dreams. We do this by providing straight talk and sound advice and always keeping their best interests in mind. We are a community bank with community values. These same principles set our standard for providing outstanding client service for more than 150 years.</p>

<h1>Switching to us is easy!</h1>
<p><strong>Let 1st Source move all of your direct deposits and automatic payments in a snap!</strong> Account information and transactions are transferred quickly in a few easy steps; it’s automated, secure and takes as little as 10 minutes. We can even help you get started closing your old account. Call <strong>800-513-2360</strong> or <a href="https://www.1stsource.com/locations"><strong>come by a branch</strong></a> to switch today! </p>
